


<div align="">

<h3>
  <samp> 
"You didn't wield it to read those papers (as you might a Kindle)."
</samp>

</div> 

</h3>

<samp> 
  Google Authenticator should not exist. Easily export OTP codes with <a href="https://github.com/dim13/otpauth">otpauth</a> and migrate today. <br> To remove cli telemetry use https://github.com/alloydwhitlock/do-not-track-cli (batteries for dodging Google's telemetry on mediapipe not included tho). <br>
  Illustration plotted with each pixel as a GFM DOM-node. Original Mozilla (1998) font modified by Shepard Fairey. Best viewed on Desktop. 
</samp>
